- **musts**: Use the `musts` CLI to validate that your changes are done. Run `musts validate` to get the validation todo list; for deterministic checks (cargo/*, bazel/build, bazel/test) run `musts run &lt;task-id&gt;` to let musts execute and record them for you; for judgment checks (agent, mav) do the work and record evidence with `musts evidence`; then re-run `musts validate` until it is empty. Use after any code change in a repo that has a `MUSTS.yml`. Also covers adding a `.mustsignore` (gitignore-style file) when local artefacts are making the validation loop noisier than it should be.
